/* V130.14.3-T1: protección limitada a la apertura de la Tienda.
 * Conserva intacto el catálogo original y sus reglas; si una lectura falla,
 * presenta el catálogo en modo consulta, con canjes bloqueados.

function protectStoreMarkup(source){
  const signature='function v30OpenStore(){';
  const boundary='\n            }\n            function setupQuickActionButtons(){';
  const begin=source.indexOf(signature);
  const finish=begin<0?-1:source.indexOf(boundary,begin);
  if(begin<0||finish<0)throw new Error('No coincide la función original de Tienda en V130.14.3');
  const endClose='\n            }';
  const originalBody=source.slice(begin+signature.length,finish);
  // Proteger exclusivamente los datos de lectura de la Tienda. Nunca autorizar
  // un canje si alguna comprobación del ciclo, historial o requisitos falla.
  let body=originalBody;
  const changes=[
    ['v32ProcessPerfectShopHistory();',
     'try{v32ProcessPerfectShopHistory();}catch(tfCycleError){tfStoreVerified=false;console.warn("TaskFlow Tienda: no se pudo comprobar ciclo",tfCycleError)}'],
    ['req=v32CurrentRequirements()',

    ['const elapsed=Math.max(0,v30DaysBetween(meta.shop.cycleStart,iso));',

/* TaskFlow T20: render no bloqueante y diagnóstico concreto de excepciones.
 * Cada vista se intenta de manera independiente; los fallos se mantienen en consola.
 * NO borra almacenamiento, no ignora excepciones críticas ni modifica las misiones. */
function tf20StabilizeCore(html){
 const a='const renderAllV31Final=renderAll;';
 const b='const updateStatsV31Final=updateStats;';
 const start=html.indexOf(a),stop=start<0?-1:html.indexOf(b,start);
 if(start>=0&&stop>start){
  const newBlock=String.raw`
            const renderAllV31Final=renderAll;
            let __tfRendering=false;
            renderAll=function(){
              if(__tfRendering)return;
              __tfRendering=true;
              try{
                const run=(name,fn)=>{
                  try{fn();return true}
                  catch(error){
                    console.error('TaskFlow T20 - actualización '+name,error);
                    try{window.__tfT20LastRenderError={area:name,message:String(error&&error.message||error).slice(0,250),time:Date.now()}}catch(_){}
                    return false;
                  }
                };
                // Errores en una sección opcional no abortan las demás vistas.
                run('metadatos',()=>v32EnsureMeta());
                run('ciclo de Tienda',()=>v32ProcessPerfectShopHistory());
                run('vista principal',()=>renderAllV31Final());
                run('accesos',()=>setupQuickActionButtons());
                run('racha',()=>v30UpgradeStreakCard());
                run('misiones obligatorias',()=>v32RenderMandatoryMissions());
                run('rutinas de pensamiento',()=>v69RenderDailyMindRoutines());
                run('Mentalista',()=>v94RenderMentalistRoutine());
                run('rango',()=>v32UpdateLobbyRankLogo());
              }finally{__tfRendering=false;}
            };
            `;
  html=html.slice(0,start)+newBlock+html.slice(stop);
 }else{console.warn('TaskFlow T20: rutina renderAll no coincide; se conserva intacta')}
 // La alerta original ocultaba el mensaje técnico de todas las excepciones.
 // Se conserva el aviso real, pero ahora indica causa y se evitan diagnósticos inventados.
 // T21: el navegador oculta detalles de scripts cruzando orígenes si no llevan CORS.
 // Conservar las excepciones identificables; almacenar avisos opacos para diagnóstico.
 const opaque="console.error('TaskFlow global error',event.error||event.message);";
 if(html.includes(opaque))html=html.replace(opaque,opaque+String.raw`
            if(String(event.message||'')==='Script error.'&&!event.error && document.querySelector('.top-bar') && document.querySelector('.wrap')){
              try{(window.__tf21OpaqueErrors||(window.__tf21OpaqueErrors=[])).push({source:String(event.filename||'fuente externa no identificada'),time:Date.now()})}catch(_){}
              return;
            }`);
 const errorText="showRecoveryNotice('Se aisló un error de ejecución. Tus datos permanecen disponibles.');";
 const rejectionText="showRecoveryNotice('Se aisló una operación incompleta sin cerrar el sistema.');";
 if(html.includes(errorText)){
  html=html.replace(errorText,String.raw`showRecoveryNotice('Error de ejecución: '+String(event.error&&event.error.message||event.message||'sin detalle').slice(0,170));`);
 }
 if(html.includes(rejectionText)){
  html=html.replace(rejectionText,String.raw`showRecoveryNotice('Operación incompleta: '+String(event.reason&&event.reason.message||event.reason||'sin detalle').slice(0,170));`);
 }
 return html;
}
function releaseLegacyCountdownMarkup(html){
 const start=html.indexOf('function updateResetCountdown() {');
 if(start<0)return html;
 const stop=html.indexOf('function stopResetCountdown()',start);
 if(stop<0)return html;
 const middle=html.slice(start,stop);
 const comment=middle.lastIndexOf('// V63');
 if(comment<0)return html;
 return html.slice(0,start)+'function updateResetCountdown() { /* T18: escritura visual propiedad de un solo reloj */ }\n            '+middle.slice(comment)+html.slice(stop);
}

/* T58 · La lógica diaria se calcula según la zona IANA vigente, sin cambiar datos de usuario. */
function tfRegionalizeHabitCycleSource(html){
 const dateFmt=/const __tfGuatemalaDateTimeFormatter = new Intl\.DateTimeFormat\('en-CA', \{[\s\S]*?\}\);/;
 const weekFmt=/const __tfGuatemalaWeekdayFormatter = new Intl\.DateTimeFormat\('en-US', \{[\s\S]*?\}\);/;
 const oldDay='if (now.hour < 1) logicalDate.setDate(logicalDate.getDate() - 1);';
 const oldKey='return `${y}-${m}-${d}-GT-01`;';
 const oldText='Hábitos se reinician a la 1:00 AM · Guatemala';
 const valid=(html.match(/const __tfGuatemalaDateTimeFormatter =/g)||[]).length===1
 &&(html.match(/const __tfGuatemalaWeekdayFormatter =/g)||[]).length===1
 &&html.includes(oldDay)&&html.includes(oldKey)&&html.includes(oldText);
 if(!valid){console.warn('TaskFlow: no se pudo adaptar la firma del ciclo diario sin modificar el núcleo');return html}
 const zone="(window.TaskFlowRegionalClock&&window.TaskFlowRegionalClock.getTimeZone&&window.TaskFlowRegionalClock.getTimeZone())||Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone||'UTC'";
 const dt="const __tfGuatemalaDateTimeFormatter={formatToParts(date){const tz="+zone+";return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:tz,year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',second:'2-digit',hourCycle:'h23'}).formatToParts(date)}};";
 const wk="const __tfGuatemalaWeekdayFormatter={format(date){const tz="+zone+";return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US',{timeZone:tz,weekday:'long'}).format(date)}};";
 return html.replace(dateFmt,dt).replace(weekFmt,wk)
 .replace(oldDay,'/* Medianoche local: el día no se retrocede a la 01:00. */')
 .replace(oldKey,'return `${y}-${m}-${d}-LOCAL-00`;')
 .replace(oldText,'Hábitos se reinician a las 12:00 AM · Hora local');
}

function prepareLoader(source){
  // Los módulos ya residen en memoria. No volver a serializar megabytes de JavaScript en el cargador.
  window.__tfT57BootPatches={protectStoreMarkup,releaseLegacyCountdownMarkup,tf20StabilizeCore,tfRegionalizeHabitCycleSource,views:TF_T37_NATIVE_VIEW_JS+'\n'+TF_T38_ICON_LIBRARY_JS+'\n'+TF_T39_NOTICE_NATIVE_JS,baseTag,headExtras,permissionPrefix:TF_T52_PERMISSION_PREFIX,bodyExtras};
  const dom=new DOMParser().parseFromString(source,'text/html');
  const parts=[...dom.querySelectorAll('script:not([src])')];
  const entry=parts.find(el=>el.textContent.includes('const expectedHash=')&&el.textContent.includes('function boot()'));
  if(!entry)throw new Error('No se encontró el cargador original de V130.14.3');
  let js=entry.textContent;
  const baseNeedle="const base='./taskflow-v99-deploy/';";
  const writeNeedle='document.open();document.write(html);document.close();';
  if(js.split(baseNeedle).length!==2||js.split(writeNeedle).length!==2)
    throw new Error('El cargador cambió y necesita revisión antes de usarlo.');
  js=js.replace(baseNeedle,"const base="+JSON.stringify(CDN+'taskflow-v99-deploy/')+";const alternateBase="+JSON.stringify(RAW+'taskflow-v99-deploy/')+";");
  // T3: desplazamiento táctil nativo; conserva los estilos y la elección de iconos.
  const bridgeNeedle='injectedStyle+cleanupScript+scrollBridgeScript+iconPortalGuard';
  if(js.includes(bridgeNeedle))js=js.replace(bridgeNeedle,'injectedStyle+cleanupScript+iconPortalGuard');
  const storeRepair='html=window.__tfT57BootPatches.tfRegionalizeHabitCycleSource(html);html=window.__tfT57BootPatches.protectStoreMarkup(html);html=window.__tfT57BootPatches.releaseLegacyCountdownMarkup(html);html=window.__tfT57BootPatches.tf20StabilizeCore(html);';
  const corsMarkupPatch=String.raw`html=html.replace(/<script\b([^>]*\bsrc=[\"'][^\"']+[\"'][^>]*)>/gi,function(full,attrs){return /\bcrossorigin\s*=/.test(attrs)?full:'<script '+attrs+' crossorigin=\"anonymous\">';});`;

function fix(){
 const panel=document.getElementById('windowContent');
 if(!panel)return;
 for(const [gridSel,cardSel,kind] of sets){
  const g=panel.querySelector(gridSel);
  if(!g)continue;
  important(g,gridRules);
  if(kind==='tf32'||kind==='tf33'){
   const holder=g.closest(kind==='tf32'?'.tf32-progress':'.tf33-progress');
   important(holder,{'display':'flex','flex-direction':'column','align-items':'stretch','width':'100%','min-width':'0','max-width':'100%',
    'height':'auto','min-height':'0','max-height':'none','padding-left':'12px','padding-right':'12px','box-sizing':'border-box','overflow':'hidden'});
   important(g,{'margin-top':'clamp(46px,13vw,76px)','width':'100%','max-width':'100%','z-index':'6'});
  }
  const cards=Array.from(g.children).filter(el=>el.matches(cardSel));
  for(const card of cards){
   important(card,cardRules);
   const icon=card.querySelector('.'+kind+'-stat-icon');
   important(icon,{'display':'grid','place-items':'center','align-self':'center','justify-self':'center','grid-column':'auto','grid-row':'auto',
    'flex-shrink':'0','margin':'0 auto','transform':'none'});
   const body=card.querySelector('.'+kind+'-stat-body');
   if(body)important(body,bodyRules);
   const head=card.querySelector('.tfm-stat-head');
   if(head)important(head,bodyRules);
   for(const node of card.querySelectorAll('b,strong,small,span:not(.tf15-stat-icon):not(.tf21-stat-icon):not(.tf32-stat-icon):not(.tf33-stat-icon):not(.tfm-stat-icon):not(.tf32-stat-body):not(.tf33-stat-body)')){
    if(node.closest('svg'))continue;
    important(node,textRules);
    if(node.matches('b,strong'))important(node,{'font-size':'clamp(12px,2.7vw,18px)','font-weight':'800','line-height':'1.22'});
    else important(node,{'font-size':'clamp(10px,2vw,12px)','line-height':'1.32'});
   }
   if((kind==='tf21'||kind==='tf15'||kind==='tfm')&&card===cards[2]){
    const value=card.querySelector('b,strong');
    important(value,{'font-size':'clamp(11px,2.25vw,16px)','line-height':'1.24'});
   }
  }
 }
 // V131: las tres rutinas se distribuyen en su controlador exclusivo.
}
let pending=false;
function schedule(){
 if(pending)return;
 pending=true;
 requestAnimationFrame(()=>{pending=false;fix()});
}
function boot(){
 const panel=document.getElementById('windowContent');
 if(!panel)return;
 new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(panel,{childList:true,subtree:true});
 const outer=document.getElementById('windowContainer');
 if(outer)new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(outer,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
 schedule();
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

<script id="tf26RuleSeparationScript">
(function(){
'use strict';
if(window.__tf26LegalRuleFix)return;window.__tf26LegalRuleFix=true;
const panel=document.getElementById('windowContent');
const outer=document.getElementById('windowContainer');
if(!panel||!outer)return;
let queued=false;
function update(){
 queued=false;
 if(!outer.classList.contains('open')||!panel.classList.contains('tf21-legal'))return;
 const list=panel.querySelector('.v83-legal-list.v97-routine-scroll');
 const rule=panel.querySelector('.tf21-rule');
 if(!list||!rule)return;
 if(rule.parentElement!==list||list.firstElementChild!==rule){list.insertBefore(rule,list.firstChild);}
}
function schedule(){if(!queued){queued=true;requestAnimationFrame(update);}}
const ob=new MutationObserver(records=>{
 if(records.some(r=>r.type==='childList'))schedule();
});
ob.observe(panel,{childList:true,subtree:false});
const cls=new MutationObserver(schedule);
cls.observe(outer,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
document.addEventListener('click',schedule,{capture:true,passive:true});
schedule();
})();
</script>

<script id="tf21LaunchArrowV137JS">
(function(){
 'use strict';
 if(window.__tf21LaunchArrowV137)return;
 window.__tf21LaunchArrowV137=true;
 function clean(){
  const panel=document.getElementById('windowContent');
  if(!panel||!panel.classList.contains('tf21-legal'))return;
  panel.querySelectorAll('.tf21-launch > .tf21-next').forEach(el=>el.remove());
 }
 function boot(){
  const panel=document.getElementById('windowContent');
  if(!panel)return;
  new MutationObserver(clean).observe(panel,{subtree:true,childList:true});
  new MutationObserver(clean).observe(panel,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  clean();
 }
 if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});
 else boot();
})();
</script>

<script id="tf21WebViewFitV140">
/* TaskFlow V140: ajuste activo únicamente para progreso jurídico en Android WebView. */
(function(){
'use strict';
if(window.__tf21WebViewFitV140)return;
window.__tf21WebViewFitV140=true;
function start(){
 const panel=document.getElementById('windowContent'),modal=document.getElementById('windowContainer');
 if(!panel||!modal)return;
 const webview=/\bwv\b|WebView|AppCreator24|Version\/4\.0/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
 let frame=0;
 function important(node,name,value){
  if(node&&(node.style.getPropertyValue(name)!==value||node.style.getPropertyPriority(name)!=='important'))
   node.style.setProperty(name,value,'important');
 }
 function textRects(node){
  if(!node||!node.isConnected)return [];
  try{const r=document.createRange();r.selectNodeContents(node);
   const result=Array.from(r.getClientRects());r.detach?.();return result;
  }catch(_){return []}
 }
 function textOutside(node,box){
  if(!node||!box)return false;
  const b=box.getBoundingClientRect();
  if(!b.width)return false;
  return textRects(node).some(r=>r.width>1&&(r.left<b.left-2||r.right>b.right+2));
 }
 function intersect(a,b){
  if(!a||!b)return false;
  const x=a.getBoundingClientRect(),y=b.getBoundingClientRect();
  return x.right>y.left+2&&y.right>x.left+2&&x.bottom>y.top+2&&y.bottom>x.top+2;
 }
 function fit(){
  if(!panel.classList.contains('tf21-legal')||!modal.classList.contains('open'))return;
  const root=panel.querySelector('.tf21-progress');
  if(!root)return;
  const head=root.querySelector('.tf21-progress-head');
  const cards=Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('.tf21-stats > .tf21-stat'));
  if(!head||cards.length!==3)return;
  const label=head.querySelector('.tf21-progress-label');
  const pill=head.querySelector('.tf21-count-pill');
  const pct=head.querySelector('.tf21-pct');
  const statsBad=cards.some(card=>{
   const body=card.querySelector('.tf21-stat-body');
   return !!body&&(body.scrollWidth>body.clientWidth+2||
    Array.from(body.children).some(el=>textOutside(el,card)));
  });
  const headBad=intersect(label,pill)||intersect(pill,pct)||
   Array.from(label?.children||[]).some(el=>pill&&textRects(el).some(r=>{
    const p=pill.getBoundingClientRect();
    return r.right>p.left+2&&r.left<p.right-2&&r.bottom>p.top+2&&r.top<p.bottom-2;
   }));
  if(!webview&&!statsBad&&!headBad)return;
  for(const [index,card] of cards.entries()){
   const body=card.querySelector('.tf21-stat-body');
   if(!body)continue;
   const style=getComputedStyle(card);
   const inner=Math.max(50,card.getBoundingClientRect().width-
    (parseFloat(style.paddingLeft)||0)-(parseFloat(style.paddingRight)||0)-2);
   important(card,'min-width','0px');important(card,'max-width','100%');important(card,'height','auto');
   important(body,'position','static');important(body,'min-width','0px');
   important(body,'max-width','100%');important(body,'width','100%');important(body,'overflow','visible');
   const texts=Array.from(body.children).filter(el=>el.matches('b,strong,span,small'));
   texts.forEach((el,i)=>{
    const primary=i===0;
    const size=primary
      ? (index===2?Math.min(15,Math.max(10,inner/8.4)):
         Math.min(19,Math.max(12,inner/6.8)))
      : Math.min(11,Math.max(8,inner/9.5));
    important(el,'display','block');important(el,'position','static');
    important(el,'max-width','100%');important(el,'width','100%');
    important(el,'white-space','normal');important(el,'overflow-wrap','anywhere');
    important(el,'word-break','normal');important(el,'text-overflow','clip');
    important(el,'font-size',size.toFixed(2)+'px');
    important(el,'line-height',primary?'1.18':'1.25');
    let current=size;
    for(let j=0;j<7&&textOutside(el,card)&&current>7.5;j++){
     current=Math.max(7.5,current*.88);
     important(el,'font-size',current.toFixed(2)+'px');
    }
   });
  }
  if(webview||headBad){
   const width=head.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   const secondRow=headBad||width<390;
   const colIcon=Math.min(40,Math.max(30,width*.1));
   const colPct=Math.min(50,Math.max(34,width*.11));
   important(head,'display','grid');important(head,'min-width','0px');
   important(head,'width','100%');important(head,'height','auto');important(head,'gap','6px 7px');
   important(label,'min-width','0px');important(label,'max-width','100%');
   const title=label?.querySelector('strong');
   const subtitle=label?.querySelector('small');
   if(title){important(title,'font-size',Math.min(15,Math.max(11,width/34)).toFixed(2)+'px');important(title,'white-space','normal');important(title,'overflow-wrap','anywhere')}
   if(subtitle){important(subtitle,'font-size',Math.min(11,Math.max(9,width/52)).toFixed(2)+'px');important(subtitle,'white-space','normal');important(subtitle,'overflow-wrap','anywhere')}
   if(pill){
    important(pill,'position','static');important(pill,'min-width','0px');
    important(pill,'max-width','100%');important(pill,'height','auto');
    const count=pill.querySelector('b,strong'),caption=pill.querySelector('small,span');
    if(count){important(count,'font-size','16px');important(count,'line-height','1.1')}
    if(caption){important(caption,'font-size','8px');important(caption,'line-height','1.2');important(caption,'white-space','normal');important(caption,'overflow-wrap','anywhere')}
   }
   if(pct){important(pct,'position','static');important(pct,'white-space','nowrap');important(pct,'font-size',Math.min(28,Math.max(19,width/15)).toFixed(2)+'px')}
   if(secondRow){
    important(head,'grid-template-columns',colIcon+'px minmax(0,1fr) '+colPct+'px');
    important(head,'grid-template-rows','auto auto');
    if(label){important(label,'grid-column','2');important(label,'grid-row','1')}
    if(pct){important(pct,'grid-column','3');important(pct,'grid-row','1')}
    if(pill){important(pill,'grid-column','2 / 4');important(pill,'grid-row','2');important(pill,'justify-self','start');important(pill,'width','auto')}
   }else{
    const pillW=Math.max(72,Math.min(102,width*.17));
    important(head,'grid-template-columns',colIcon+'px minmax(0,1fr) '+pillW+'px '+colPct+'px');
    important(head,'grid-template-rows','auto');
    if(label){important(label,'grid-column','2');important(label,'grid-row','1')}
    if(pill){important(pill,'grid-column','3');important(pill,'grid-row','1');important(pill,'width','100%')}
    if(pct){important(pct,'grid-column','4');important(pct,'grid-row','1')}
    if(label&&pill&&Array.from(label.children).some(el=>textRects(el).some(r=>{
     const p=pill.getBoundingClientRect();
     return r.right>p.left-1&&r.bottom>p.top+1&&r.top<p.bottom-1;
    }))){
     important(head,'grid-template-columns',colIcon+'px minmax(0,1fr) '+colPct+'px');
     important(head,'grid-template-rows','auto auto');
     important(pill,'grid-column','2 / 4');important(pill,'grid-row','2');
     important(pill,'width','auto');important(pill,'justify-self','start');
     if(pct)important(pct,'grid-column','3');
    }
   }
  }
 }
 function schedule(){if(frame)return;frame=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{frame=0;fit()})}
 const changes=new MutationObserver(records=>{
  if(records.some(m=>m.type==='childList'||m.type==='characterData'))schedule();
 });
 changes.observe(panel,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true});
 changes.observe(modal,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
 if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){const resize=new ResizeObserver(schedule);resize.observe(panel)}
 window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
 window.addEventListener('pageshow',schedule,{passive:true});
 schedule();
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start,{once:true});else start();
})();

<script id="tfPortraitWebViewFitV141">
/* TaskFlow V141 — ajustar exclusivamente el progreso de Sócrates y Maquiavelo en WebView. */
(function(){
'use strict';
if(window.__tfPortraitProgressFitV141)return;
window.__tfPortraitProgressFitV141=true;
const viewKinds=[
 {panel:'tf32-socrates',key:'tf32'},
 {panel:'tf33-machiavelli',key:'tf33'}
];
function start(){
 const panel=document.getElementById('windowContent');
 const outer=document.getElementById('windowContainer');
 if(!panel||!outer)return;
 const webview=/\bwv\b|WebView|AppCreator24|Version\/4\.0/i.test(String(navigator.userAgent||''));
 let frame=0;
 function set(node,property,value){
  if(!node)return;
  if(node.style.getPropertyValue(property)!==value||
     node.style.getPropertyPriority(property)!=='important')
   node.style.setProperty(property,value,'important');
 }
 function bounds(node){
  if(!node?.isConnected)return [];
  try{
   const range=document.createRange();
   range.selectNodeContents(node);
   const items=Array.from(range.getClientRects());
   range.detach?.();
   return items;
  }catch(_){return []}
 }
 function spills(node,box){
  if(!node||!box)return false;
  const rect=box.getBoundingClientRect();
  if(rect.width<1)return false;
  return bounds(node).some(r=>r.width>1&&(
    r.left<rect.left-2||r.right>rect.right+2));
 }
 function adjustText(node,box,size,minSize,maxSize,primary){
  if(!node)return;
  set(node,'box-sizing','border-box');
  set(node,'display','block');
  set(node,'position','static');
  set(node,'left','auto');
  set(node,'width','100%');
  set(node,'min-width','0');
  set(node,'max-width','100%');
  set(node,'height','auto');
  set(node,'min-height','0');
  set(node,'white-space','normal');
  set(node,'overflow-wrap','anywhere');
  set(node,'word-break','normal');
  set(node,'text-overflow','clip');
  set(node,'transform','none');
  set(node,'letter-spacing','normal');
  set(node,'line-height',primary?'1.22':'1.3');
  let current=Math.min(maxSize,Math.max(minSize,size));
  set(node,'font-size',current.toFixed(2)+'px');
  for(let attempt=0;attempt<9&&spills(node,box)&&current>minSize;attempt++){
   current=Math.max(minSize,current*.88);
   set(node,'font-size',current.toFixed(2)+'px');
  }
 }
 function cardOverflow(card,key){
  const body=card.querySelector('.'+key+'-stat-body');
  if(!body)return false;
  return body.scrollWidth>body.clientWidth+2||
   Array.from(body.querySelectorAll('b,strong,small,span')).some(n=>spills(n,card));
 }
 function fitView(){
  if(!outer.classList.contains('open'))return;
  const type=viewKinds.find(v=>panel.classList.contains(v.panel));
  if(!type)return;
  const key=type.key;
  const progress=panel.querySelector(':scope > .'+key+'-progress');
  if(!progress||!progress.getBoundingClientRect().width)return;
  const grid=progress.querySelector('.'+key+'-stats');
  const cards=grid?Array.from(grid.children).filter(n=>n.classList.contains(key+'-stat')):[];
  const copy=progress.querySelector('.'+key+'-progress-copy');
  const percent=progress.querySelector('.'+key+'-percent');
  if(cards.length!==3||!copy)return;
  const title=copy.querySelector('strong');
  const subtitle=copy.querySelector('small');
  const isBad=cards.some(card=>cardOverflow(card,key))||
    [title,subtitle].some(el=>spills(el,copy))||
    spills(percent,progress);
  if(!webview&&!isBad)return;
  // Aplicar solo cuando la vista es WebView o se detecta un desbordamiento real.
  set(progress,'-webkit-text-size-adjust','none');
  set(progress,'text-size-adjust','none');
  set(copy,'-webkit-text-size-adjust','none');
  set(copy,'text-size-adjust','none');
  set(copy,'min-width','0');
  set(copy,'height','auto');
  set(copy,'max-height','none');
  set(copy,'overflow','visible');
  const copyWidth=copy.getBoundingClientRect().width;
  for(const [node,primary] of [[title,true],[subtitle,false]]){
   if(!node)continue;
   set(node,'-webkit-text-size-adjust','none');
   set(node,'text-size-adjust','none');
   // Los titulares usan únicamente el espacio libre junto al retrato existente.
   adjustText(node,copy,
    primary?copyWidth/12:copyWidth/18,
    primary?12:9,
    primary?23:14,
    primary);
   set(node,'text-align','left');
  }
  if(percent){
   set(percent,'-webkit-text-size-adjust','none');
   set(percent,'text-size-adjust','none');
   set(percent,'position','relative');
   set(percent,'inset','auto');
   set(percent,'transform','none');
   set(percent,'min-width','0');
   set(percent,'max-width','67%');
   set(percent,'white-space','nowrap');
   const width=progress.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   set(percent,'font-size',Math.min(39,Math.max(23,width/12)).toFixed(2)+'px');
   set(percent,'line-height','1.16');
  }
  set(grid,'-webkit-text-size-adjust','none');
  set(grid,'text-size-adjust','none');
  set(grid,'display','grid');
  set(grid,'grid-template-columns','repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr))');
  set(grid,'grid-auto-rows','minmax(136px,auto)');
  set(grid,'width','100%');
  set(grid,'min-width','0');
  set(grid,'max-width','100%');
  set(grid,'height','auto');
  set(grid,'max-height','none');
  set(grid,'overflow','visible');
  // Mantener el margen original que separa las tarjetas del retrato.
  cards.forEach((card,index)=>{
   set(card,'-webkit-text-size-adjust','none');
   set(card,'text-size-adjust','none');
   set(card,'box-sizing','border-box');
   set(card,'min-width','0');
   set(card,'max-width','100%');
   set(card,'width','100%');
   set(card,'height','auto');
   set(card,'max-height','none');
   set(card,'overflow','hidden');
   const icon=card.querySelector('.'+key+'-stat-icon');
   if(icon){set(icon,'flex-shrink','0');set(icon,'max-width','100%')}
   const body=card.querySelector('.'+key+'-stat-body');
   if(!body)return;
   set(body,'-webkit-text-size-adjust','none');
   set(body,'text-size-adjust','none');
   set(body,'position','relative');
   set(body,'left','-18px');
   set(body,'width','100%');
   set(body,'min-width','0');
   set(body,'max-width','100%');
   set(body,'height','auto');
   set(body,'max-height','none');
   set(body,'overflow','visible');
   set(body,'box-sizing','border-box');
   const box=card.getBoundingClientRect();
   const style=getComputedStyle(card);
   const inner=Math.max(42,box.width-
    (parseFloat(style.paddingLeft)||0)-
    (parseFloat(style.paddingRight)||0)-2);
   const labels=Array.from(body.children).filter(n=>n.matches('b,strong,small,span'));
   labels.forEach(label=>{
    const primary=label.matches('b,strong');
    const size=primary?Math.min(index===2?15:17,inner/(index===2?7.5:6.8)):
      Math.min(11,inner/9.5);
    set(label,'-webkit-text-size-adjust','none');
    set(label,'text-size-adjust','none');
    adjustText(label,card,size,primary?10:7.5,primary?(index===2?15:17):11,primary);
    set(label,'text-align','center');
   });
  });
 }
 function schedule(){
  if(frame)return;
  frame=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{frame=0;fitView()});
 }
 const nodes=new MutationObserver(records=>{
  if(records.some(r=>r.type==='childList'||r.type==='characterData'))schedule();
 });
 nodes.observe(panel,{subtree:true,childList:true,characterData:true});
 const mode=new MutationObserver(schedule);
 mode.observe(panel,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
 mode.observe(outer,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
 if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){
  const ro=new ResizeObserver(schedule);
  ro.observe(panel);
 }
 window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
 window.addEventListener('orientationchange',schedule,{passive:true});
 window.addEventListener('pageshow',schedule,{passive:true});
 schedule();
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start,{once:true});
else start();
})();

<script id="tfPortraitCardsContainV142">
/* TaskFlow V142. Sócrates y Maquiavelo: tarjetas adaptables y contenidas en WebView. */
(function(){
'use strict';
if(window.__tfPortraitCardsContainV142)return;
window.__tfPortraitCardsContainV142=true;
const modes=[['tf32-socrates','tf32'],['tf33-machiavelli','tf33']];
function boot(){
 const panel=document.getElementById('windowContent');
 const outer=document.getElementById('windowContainer');
 if(!panel||!outer)return;
 let queued=false;
 function put(el,p,v){
  if(el&&(el.style.getPropertyValue(p)!==v||el.style.getPropertyPriority(p)!=='important')){
   el.style.setProperty(p,v,'important');
  }
 }
 function set(el,props){
  if(!el)return;
  for(const [p,v] of Object.entries(props))put(el,p,v);
 }
 function fitText(el,max,first){
  if(!el)return;
  set(el,{
   'position':'static','left':'auto','display':'block','box-sizing':'border-box',
   'width':'100%','min-width':'0','max-width':'100%',
   'height':'auto','min-height':'0','max-height':'none',
   'white-space':'normal','overflow-wrap':'anywhere','word-break':'normal',
   'text-overflow':'clip','transform':'none','letter-spacing':'normal',
   'text-align':'center','line-height':first?'1.18':'1.26',
   '-webkit-text-size-adjust':'none','text-size-adjust':'none'
  });
  const size=Math.min(first?14:10,Math.max(first?10:8,max/(first?5.7:8.2)));
  put(el,'font-size',size.toFixed(2)+'px');
  // Nunca truncar las palabras largas: reducir solo si todavía exceden la tarjeta.
  let s=size;
  for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
   const parent=el.closest('.tf32-stat,.tf33-stat');
   if(!parent)break;
   const a=parent.getBoundingClientRect();
   let escapes=false;
   try{
    const range=document.createRange();
    range.selectNodeContents(el);
    escapes=Array.from(range.getClientRects()).some(r=>r.width>0&&(r.left<a.left-2||r.right>a.right+2));
    range.detach?.();
   }catch(_){}
   if(!escapes||s<=7.5)break;
   s=Math.max(7.5,s*.87);
   put(el,'font-size',s.toFixed(2)+'px');
  }
 }
 function apply(){
  if(!outer.classList.contains('open'))return;
  const mode=modes.find(item=>panel.classList.contains(item[0]));
  if(!mode)return;
  const key=mode[1],progress=panel.querySelector(':scope > .'+key+'-progress');
  if(!progress)return;
  const grid=progress.querySelector('.'+key+'-stats');
  if(!grid)return;
  const cards=Array.from(grid.children).filter(n=>n.classList.contains(key+'-stat'));
  if(cards.length!==3)return;
  const compact=window.innerWidth<=900 || /(?:\bwv\b|WebView|AppCreator24|Version\/4\.0)/i.test(navigator.userAgent) ||
    cards.some(c=>c.getBoundingClientRect().width<155);
  if(!compact)return;
  set(progress,{
   'box-sizing':'border-box','width':'100%','min-width':'0','max-width':'100%',
   'height':'auto','min-height':'0','max-height':'none',
   'overflow':'hidden','-webkit-text-size-adjust':'none','text-size-adjust':'none'
  });
  set(grid,{
   'display':'grid','grid-template-columns':'repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr))',
   'grid-auto-rows':'minmax(80px,auto)','align-items':'stretch','gap':'5px',
   'box-sizing':'border-box','min-width':'0','max-width':'100%',
   'height':'auto','min-height':'0','max-height':'none',
   'overflow':'visible','-webkit-text-size-adjust':'none','text-size-adjust':'none'
  });
  for(const card of cards){
   set(card,{
    'display':'flex','flex-direction':'column','align-items':'center','justify-content':'center',
    'grid-template-columns':'none','grid-column':'auto','grid-row':'auto',
    'position':'relative','inset':'auto','float':'none','flex-wrap':'nowrap',
    'box-sizing':'border-box','width':'100%','min-width':'0','max-width':'100%',
    'height':'auto','min-height':'80px','max-height':'none',
    'padding':'7px 3px','margin':'0','gap':'4px','overflow':'hidden',
    'text-align':'center','transform':'none',
    '-webkit-text-size-adjust':'none','text-size-adjust':'none'
   });
   const icon=card.querySelector('.'+key+'-stat-icon');
   set(icon,{
    'display':'grid','place-items':'center','position':'relative','inset':'auto',
    'align-self':'center','justify-self':'center','flex':'0 0 auto',
    'grid-column':'auto','grid-row':'auto',
    'width':'24px','height':'24px','min-width':'0','min-height':'0','max-width':'100%',
    'margin':'0 auto','transform':'none'
   });
   const body=card.querySelector('.'+key+'-stat-body');
   if(!body)continue;
   set(body,{
    'display':'flex','flex-direction':'column','align-items':'center','justify-content':'center',
    'position':'relative','inset':'auto','left':'-18px','grid-column':'auto','grid-row':'auto',
    'box-sizing':'border-box','flex':'0 1 auto','width':'100%','min-width':'0','max-width':'100%',
    'height':'auto','min-height':'0','max-height':'none',
    'padding':'0','margin':'0','gap':'2px','overflow':'visible',
    'text-align':'center','transform':'none',
    '-webkit-text-size-adjust':'none','text-size-adjust':'none'
   });
   const computed=getComputedStyle(card),w=Math.max(30,card.getBoundingClientRect().width-
      (parseFloat(computed.paddingLeft)||0)-(parseFloat(computed.paddingRight)||0)-4);
   for(const leaf of Array.from(body.children).filter(n=>n.matches('b,strong,small,span'))){
    fitText(leaf,w,leaf.matches('b,strong'));
   }
  }
 }
 function schedule(){
  if(queued)return;
  queued=true;
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{queued=false;apply()});
 }
 const mutations=new MutationObserver(events=>{
  if(events.some(e=>e.type==='childList'||e.type==='characterData'||
     (e.type==='attributes'&&e.attributeName==='style'&&
      e.target.closest?.('.tf32-progress,.tf33-progress'))))schedule();
 });
 mutations.observe(panel,{subtree:true,childList:true,characterData:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['style']});
 const cls=new MutationObserver(schedule);
 cls.observe(panel,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
 cls.observe(outer,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
 if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){
  const ro=new ResizeObserver(schedule);ro.observe(panel);
 }
 window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
 window.addEventListener('orientationchange',schedule,{passive:true});
 window.addEventListener('pageshow',schedule,{passive:true});
 schedule();
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});
else boot();
})();
</script>
